- **update-llms-txt**: Invoke with `/project:update-llms-txt` on any branch whose diff could change what `public/llms.txt` says. That file is a hand-curated, machine-readable summary for AI agents and LLM crawlers (the [llms.txt convention](https://llmstxt.org/) — unrelated to `robots.txt`, which controls crawler *access*). It is deliberately shorter and more opinionated than a docs dump, so it is edited, not generated ([answerLoops/answerLoops](https://github.com/answerLoops/answerLoops/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/update-llms-txt.md) · ★29)
